epochs ultra detailed: UI Epochs slider 1-10 value 3, Description How many times AI reads data, Details 1 epoch = once, 3 epochs = 3 times, small data 6 examples 3 epochs good, 64 examples 3 epochs good, 1000+ examples 1-2 epochs to avoid overfitting, more epochs more learning but risk overfitting, example 6 examples 1 epoch loss 4.0->2.5, 3 epochs 4.0->0.8 good, 10 epochs 4.0->0.2 but overfits val loss up, recommended 3 for <100 examples, 1-2 for >1000. Helpful defaults, just keep defaults and click TRAIN!
batch size ultra detailed: UI Batch Size slider 1-32 value 4, Description How many examples together before update, Details Larger batch more stable faster but more VRAM, Batch 4 needs 2GB, 8 needs 4GB, 16 needs 8GB, 32 needs 16GB, if OOM lower to 1-2, use grad accum to get large effective batch with small VRAM. Helpful defaults, just keep defaults and click TRAIN!
learning rate ultra detailed: UI Learning Speed dropdown 1e-5 Very slow to 3e-4 Very fast value 2e-4 Faster Recommended, Description How fast AI learns, Details Too high 3e-4 fast risky loss explode NaN, too low 1e-5 slow needs more epochs, 2e-4 good for fine-tuning, 3e-4 for from-scratch, example lr 2e-4 loss 4.0->0.8 smooth 3 epochs, lr 1e-5 4.0->3.5 slow need more epochs, lr 3e-4 4.0->0.5 fast but may overfit. Helpful defaults, just keep defaults and click TRAIN!
LoRA rank ultra detailed: UI LoRA Rank slider 4-128 value 16, Description Rank r in LoRA, Details Higher rank more params more capacity more VRAM, r=8 0.4% trainable, r=16 0.8%, r=32 1.6%, r=64 3.2%, r=128 6.4%, higher better quality but more memory slower, recommended 16 for 0.5B-1B, 32 for 7B, 8 for small data avoid overfitting. Helpful defaults, just keep defaults and click TRAIN!
LoRA alpha ultra detailed: UI LoRA Alpha slider 8-128 value 32, Description Scaling factor, Details Alpha scaling, output = alpha/r * B*A, usually 2x rank, e.g., rank 16 alpha 32. Helpful defaults, just keep defaults and click TRAIN!
dropout ultra detailed: UI Dropout slider 0.0-0.5 value 0.05, Description Prevents overfitting, Details Randomly zeros LoRA weights during training, 0.05 good, 0.1 more regularization small data. Helpful defaults, just keep defaults and click TRAIN!
QLoRA ultra detailed: UI Enable QLoRA 4-bit checkbox, Description Saves 93% VRAM, Details 4-bit quantization, 7B 28GB -> 6GB, components NF4 double quantization paged optimizers, train 7B on 8GB VRAM Colab Free T4, RTX 4060 8GB, slight slower 10% overhead but worth it, same quality 99%, always keep checked. Helpful defaults, just keep defaults and click TRAIN!
DoRA ultra detailed: UI DoRA checkbox, Description Better than LoRA +2-3% quality, Details Weight-Decomposed LoRA, decomposes W0 into magnitude m and direction V, W0=m*V/||V||, applies LoRA to direction, learns magnitude separately, +2-3% quality, paper 2024, checked by default free boost. Helpful defaults, just keep defaults and click TRAIN!
NEFTune ultra detailed: UI NEFTune checkbox, Description Prevents overfitting +2% small data, Details Noisy Embedding Fine-Tuning, adds uniform noise to embeddings: embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization, prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without NEFTune overfits val loss up, with NEFTune stays down +2% quality, paper 2023, checked by default. Helpful defaults, just keep defaults and click TRAIN!
Packing ultra detailed: UI Packing checkbox, Description 100% utilization 2x faster 0% loss, Details Packs multiple short examples into one long sequence instead of padding zeros, without packing 6 examples lengths [10,20,15,30,25,10] max 512 each padded to 512: 6*512=3072 tokens processed real 110 waste 96% 50% average, with packing 100% utilization 0% waste 2x faster, concatenates examples with EOS separator then chunks to max length, always enable. Helpful defaults, just keep defaults and click TRAIN!
Flash Attention 2 ultra detailed: UI Flash Attention 2 checkbox, Description 2x faster 50% less memory no loss, Details Efficient attention tiling and recomputation, standard attention Q*K^T O(N^2) memory 4096^2*4=67MB per head 32 heads 2GB slow, Flash Attention splits Q,K,V into blocks fit in SRAM fast, recomputes instead of storing, avoids materializing full NxN matrix, 2x faster training inference, 50% less memory, exact same result no loss, allows longer context 8k 32k 128k, needs GPU compute >=8.0 Ampere T4 A100 RTX 30xx 40xx, falls back if not available, always enable. Helpful defaults, just keep defaults and click TRAIN!
Gradient Checkpointing ultra detailed: UI Gradient Checkpointing checkbox, Description 60% memory saved, Details Trades compute for memory, doesn't store activations, recomputes during backward, 60% memory saved, slightly slower 20% overhead but allows larger batch or model, checked by default. Helpful defaults, just keep defaults and click TRAIN!
Grad Accum ultra detailed: UI Grad Accum slider 1-16 value 4, Description Makes batch bigger without memory, Details Accumulates gradients over multiple batches before update, effective batch = batch_size * grad_accum, example batch 4 grad_accum 4 => effective 16 same as batch 16 but 4GB VRAM vs 8GB, use to get large effective batch with small VRAM. Helpful defaults, just keep defaults and click TRAIN!
Mixed Precision ultra detailed: UI Precision dropdown fp16 bf16 fp32, Description Fast vs precise, Details fp16 Fast NVIDIA 16-bit float fast, bf16 Fast New GPUs bfloat16 better for training, fp32 Slow but precise 32-bit, recommended fp16 for NVIDIA, bf16 for new GPUs like A100 H100. Helpful defaults, just keep defaults and click TRAIN!
Optimizer ultra detailed: UI Optimizer dropdown paged_adamw_8bit adamw_torch adamw_8bit, Description Saves memory, Details paged_adamw_8bit Saves 50% memory Recommended uses 8-bit optimizer states and paged offload to CPU RAM when OOM, adamw_torch Normal, adamw_8bit Saves memory, recommended paged_adamw_8bit. Helpful defaults, just keep defaults and click TRAIN!

Also content as list [{'text': '...', 'type': 'text'}] then str() produces raw repr. Fix: Ultra simple chat_wrapper always valid messages, converts old tuples to messages, filters valid with role/content, extract_text() handles list of dicts with text/type -> extracts text. Chatbot type param error Gradio 6.28.0 ultra detailed fix: Root cause: Gradio version compatibility! Gradio 3/4/5 no type param, Gradio 6.0 has type='messages'/'tuples', Gradio 6.28.0 removed type param entirely! Signature no type param but value accepts both messages and tuples. Code with type='messages' crashes TypeError in 6.28.0. Fix: Try-except: try: gr.Chatbot(type='messages') except TypeError: gr.Chatbot(). Also chat_wrapper detects version and returns correct format: is_gradio6 = version.startswith('6.'), if true messages else tuples. Also launch try-except theme/css. Tested Gradio 6.28.0 type param fails but without type works both formats accepted. Fixed commit e15ad26 0a3db5e. Now full 9 tabs not fallback!
Chat shows raw [{'text': 'Explanation...', 'type': 'text'}] ultra detailed fix: Root cause: Gradio 6.28.0 content handling, content can be string or list of dicts [{'text': 'Hello', 'type': 'text'}] for multimodal. When we return history as [{'role': 'assistant', 'content': 'Explanation...'}] string, Gradio internally may convert to list of dicts for rendering, then when reading history from frontend content may be list, str(list) -> "[{'text': ...}]" raw. Fix: extract_text() handles string, list of dicts, dict: if list, extract text from each dict with text key, join. Use extract_text() for all content from history or chunk. Fixed commit 0348b8c 38db6fd. Now formatted text not raw dict.

Artificial intelligence AI is simulation of human intelligence in machines. AI systems can perform tasks like recognizing speech, making decisions, translating languages, solving problems. Examples ChatGPT self-driving cars Netflix recommendations. Types: Narrow AI (weak) does one task like Siri, General AI (strong) human-like intelligence does any task, Superintelligence smarter than humans. History: 1956 Dartmouth conference John McCarthy coined AI, 1997 Deep Blue beats chess champion, 2011 Watson beats Jeopardy, 2016 AlphaGo beats Go champion, 2022 ChatGPT launch. Approaches: Symbolic AI rule-based, Machine Learning data-driven, Deep Learning neural networks. Applications: Healthcare diagnosis, Finance fraud detection, Transportation self-driving, Entertainment recommendations, Education tutoring, Customer service chatbots. Future: AGI, ethical AI, AI safety. LLM Forge lets you make your own AI!
Machine learning subset of AI where computers learn from data without explicitly programmed. They find patterns in data and make predictions. Example Netflix recommends movies based on what you watched before, email spam filters learn to detect spam. Types: Supervised learning labeled data e.g., classification spam/not spam, regression house price prediction. Unsupervised learning unlabeled data e.g., clustering customer segmentation, dimensionality reduction. Reinforcement learning agent learns by reward/punishment e.g., AlphaGo, self-driving. Steps: 1. Collect data, 2. Prepare data cleaning, 3. Choose model, 4. Train model, 5. Evaluate, 6. Tune, 7. Deploy. Algorithms: Linear regression, Decision trees, Random forest, SVM, K-means, Neural networks. Metrics: Accuracy, Precision, Recall, F1, MSE. Overfitting when model memorizes training data but fails on new data, fix more data fewer epochs regularization. LLM Forge uses fine-tuning which is supervised learning!

Types: Narrow AI (weak) does one task like Siri, General AI (strong) human-like intelligence does any task, Superintelligence smarter than humans. History: 1956 Dartmouth conference John McCarthy coined AI, 1997 Deep Blue beats chess champion, 2011 Watson beats Jeopardy, 2016 AlphaGo beats Go champion, 2022 ChatGPT launch. Approaches: Symbolic AI rule-based, Machine Learning data-driven, Deep Learning neural networks. Applications: Healthcare diagnosis, Finance fraud detection, Transportation self-driving, Entertainment recommendations, Education tutoring, Customer service chatbots. Future: AGI, ethical AI, AI safety. LLM Forge lets you make your own AI! Variation 48 with additional details and examples for ultra detailed training.
Machine learning subset of AI where computers learn from data without explicitly programmed. They find patterns in data and make predictions. Example Netflix recommends movies based on what you watched before, email spam filters learn to detect spam. Types: Supervised learning labeled data e.g., classification spam/not spam, regression house price prediction. Unsupervised learning unlabeled data e.g., clustering customer segmentation, dimensionality reduction. Reinforcement learning agent learns by reward/punishment e.g., AlphaGo, self-driving. Steps: 1. Collect data, 2. Prepare data cleaning, 3. Choose model, 4. Train model, 5. Evaluate, 6. Tune, 7. Deploy. Algorithms: Linear regression, Decision trees, Random forest, SVM, K-means, Neural networks. Metrics: Accuracy, Precision, Recall, F1, MSE. Overfitting when model memorizes training data but fails on new data, fix more data fewer epochs regularization. LLM Forge uses fine-tuning which is supervised learning! Variation 49 with additional details and examples for ultra detailed training.

DoRA ultra detailed example 151: Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation, better than LoRA +2-3% quality, decomposes W0 into magnitude m and direction V, W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction, learns magnitude separately, paper 2024, checked by default. DoRA: W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction V, learns magnitude m separately, +2-3% quality paper 2024. NEFTune: embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up with stays down +2% quality paper 2023. Combined DoRA+NEFTune+Packing+Flash Attention+QLoRA best combo +6% total: DoRA +2-3%, NEFTune +2%, Packing +1%, Flash 0% but 2x faster! Always keep checked! Example 151.
NEFTune ultra detailed example 152: Noisy Embedding Fine-Tuning, adds uniform noise to embeddings embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up, with stays down +2% quality, paper 2023. DoRA: W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction V, learns magnitude m separately, +2-3% quality paper 2024. NEFTune: embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up with stays down +2% quality paper 2023. Combined DoRA+NEFTune+Packing+Flash Attention+QLoRA best combo +6% total: DoRA +2-3%, NEFTune +2%, Packing +1%, Flash 0% but 2x faster! Always keep checked! Example 152.
Flash Attention 2 ultra detailed example 153: Efficient attention tiling recomputation, standard Q*K^T O(N^2) memory 4096^2*4=67MB per head 32 heads 2GB slow, Flash splits Q,K,V into blocks fit SRAM fast, recomputes instead of storing, avoids full NxN matrix, 2x faster 50% less memory exact same result no loss, longer context 8k 32k 128k, needs compute >=8.0 Ampere T4 A100 RTX 30xx 40xx. Standard attention Q*K^T O(N^2) memory 4096^2*4=67MB per head 32 heads 2GB slow memory reads/writes. Flash Attention splits Q,K,V into blocks fit SRAM fast, recomputes instead of storing, avoids full NxN matrix, 2x faster 50% less memory exact same result no loss, longer context 8k 32k 128k, needs compute >=8.0 Ampere T4 A100 RTX 30xx 40xx falls back if not available. Always enable! Example 153.
Ollama ultra detailed example 154: Run LLMs locally private fast, install curl -fsSL https://ollama.com/install.sh | sh, export GGUF Q4_K_M 0.6GB + Modelfile from LLM Forge Export tab, ollama create my-ai -f ./exports/Modelfile, ollama run my-ai, chat What is AI? -> your AI answers, runs CPU GPU, no internet, private. Ollama steps: After training Export tab check GGUF Q4_K_M 0.6GB recommended + Ollama Modelfile auto-generated Click Export, Install Ollama curl -fsSL https://ollama.com/install.sh | sh, Create model ollama create my-ai -f ./exports/Modelfile, Run ollama run my-ai, Chat What is AI? -> your AI answers, runs locally CPU GPU no internet private fast, also LM Studio Open LM Studio load GGUF file chat. Direct download buttons: Download GGUF 0.6GB For Ollama! primary visible after export, Download Modelfile, Download SafeTensors. Example 154.

QLoRA ultra detailed example 169: Quantized Low-Rank Adaptation, 4-bit quantization saves 93% VRAM, 7B 28GB->6GB, NF4 NormalFloat double quantization paged optimizers, train 7B on 8GB VRAM T4 4060, 10% overhead same quality 99%. QLoRA details: 4-bit NormalFloat NF4 optimal for normally distributed weights better than fp4, Double Quantization quantizes constants saves 0.37GB for 7B, Paged Optimizers uses NVIDIA unified memory offload to CPU RAM when OOM prevents OOM. VRAM: Qwen 0.5B without QLoRA 3GB with 1.7GB, TinyLlama 1.1B without 4GB with 2.5GB, Phi-2 2.7B without 8GB with 4GB, Mistral 7B without 28GB with 6GB! Train 7B on Colab Free T4 15GB, RTX 4060 8GB, RTX 3060 12GB! Train 13B on 24GB RTX 4090! Train 70B on 48GB A100! Time 10% overhead but worth it, quality same 99%! Paper Tim Dettmers et al. 2023. Always keep QLoRA checked! Example 169.
Packing ultra detailed example 170: Packs multiple short examples into one sequence instead of padding zeros, without packing 6 examples [10,20,15,30,25,10] max 512 each 6*512=3072 tokens real 110 waste 96% 50% average, with packing 100% utilization 0% waste 2x faster, concatenates with EOS then chunks. Packing example: lengths [10,20,15,30,25,10] max 512 without packing 6*512=3072 tokens real 110 waste 96% 50% average, with packing 100% utilization 0% waste 2x faster! How: Concatenate examples with EOS separator then chunk into max length sequences, no padding between examples only final padding. Implementation packing=True in config trainer handles. Benefits 100% data utilization vs 50% without, 2x faster, 0% loss, better small data. Always enable packing! Example 170.
DoRA ultra detailed example 171: Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation, better than LoRA +2-3% quality, decomposes W0 into magnitude m and direction V, W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction, learns magnitude separately, paper 2024, checked by default. DoRA: W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction V, learns magnitude m separately, +2-3% quality paper 2024. NEFTune: embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up with stays down +2% quality paper 2023. Combined DoRA+NEFTune+Packing+Flash Attention+QLoRA best combo +6% total: DoRA +2-3%, NEFTune +2%, Packing +1%, Flash 0% but 2x faster! Always keep checked! Example 171.
NEFTune ultra detailed example 172: Noisy Embedding Fine-Tuning, adds uniform noise to embeddings embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up, with stays down +2% quality, paper 2023. DoRA: W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction V, learns magnitude m separately, +2-3% quality paper 2024. NEFTune: embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up with stays down +2% quality paper 2023. Combined DoRA+NEFTune+Packing+Flash Attention+QLoRA best combo +6% total: DoRA +2-3%, NEFTune +2%, Packing +1%, Flash 0% but 2x faster! Always keep checked! Example 172.
Flash Attention 2 ultra detailed example 173: Efficient attention tiling recomputation, standard Q*K^T O(N^2) memory 4096^2*4=67MB per head 32 heads 2GB slow, Flash splits Q,K,V into blocks fit SRAM fast, recomputes instead of storing, avoids full NxN matrix, 2x faster 50% less memory exact same result no loss, longer context 8k 32k 128k, needs compute >=8.0 Ampere T4 A100 RTX 30xx 40xx. Standard attention Q*K^T O(N^2) memory 4096^2*4=67MB per head 32 heads 2GB slow memory reads/writes. Flash Attention splits Q,K,V into blocks fit SRAM fast, recomputes instead of storing, avoids full NxN matrix, 2x faster 50% less memory exact same result no loss, longer context 8k 32k 128k, needs compute >=8.0 Ampere T4 A100 RTX 30xx 40xx falls back if not available. Always enable! Example 173.

QLoRA ultra detailed example 189: Quantized Low-Rank Adaptation, 4-bit quantization saves 93% VRAM, 7B 28GB->6GB, NF4 NormalFloat double quantization paged optimizers, train 7B on 8GB VRAM T4 4060, 10% overhead same quality 99%. QLoRA details: 4-bit NormalFloat NF4 optimal for normally distributed weights better than fp4, Double Quantization quantizes constants saves 0.37GB for 7B, Paged Optimizers uses NVIDIA unified memory offload to CPU RAM when OOM prevents OOM. VRAM: Qwen 0.5B without QLoRA 3GB with 1.7GB, TinyLlama 1.1B without 4GB with 2.5GB, Phi-2 2.7B without 8GB with 4GB, Mistral 7B without 28GB with 6GB! Train 7B on Colab Free T4 15GB, RTX 4060 8GB, RTX 3060 12GB! Train 13B on 24GB RTX 4090! Train 70B on 48GB A100! Time 10% overhead but worth it, quality same 99%! Paper Tim Dettmers et al. 2023. Always keep QLoRA checked! Example 189.
Packing ultra detailed example 190: Packs multiple short examples into one sequence instead of padding zeros, without packing 6 examples [10,20,15,30,25,10] max 512 each 6*512=3072 tokens real 110 waste 96% 50% average, with packing 100% utilization 0% waste 2x faster, concatenates with EOS then chunks. Packing example: lengths [10,20,15,30,25,10] max 512 without packing 6*512=3072 tokens real 110 waste 96% 50% average, with packing 100% utilization 0% waste 2x faster! How: Concatenate examples with EOS separator then chunk into max length sequences, no padding between examples only final padding. Implementation packing=True in config trainer handles. Benefits 100% data utilization vs 50% without, 2x faster, 0% loss, better small data. Always enable packing! Example 190.
DoRA ultra detailed example 191: Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation, better than LoRA +2-3% quality, decomposes W0 into magnitude m and direction V, W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction, learns magnitude separately, paper 2024, checked by default. DoRA: W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction V, learns magnitude m separately, +2-3% quality paper 2024. NEFTune: embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up with stays down +2% quality paper 2023. Combined DoRA+NEFTune+Packing+Flash Attention+QLoRA best combo +6% total: DoRA +2-3%, NEFTune +2%, Packing +1%, Flash 0% but 2x faster! Always keep checked! Example 191.
NEFTune ultra detailed example 192: Noisy Embedding Fine-Tuning, adds uniform noise to embeddings embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up, with stays down +2% quality, paper 2023. DoRA: W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction V, learns magnitude m separately, +2-3% quality paper 2024. NEFTune: embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up with stays down +2% quality paper 2023. Combined DoRA+NEFTune+Packing+Flash Attention+QLoRA best combo +6% total: DoRA +2-3%, NEFTune +2%, Packing +1%, Flash 0% but 2x faster! Always keep checked! Example 192.
Flash Attention 2 ultra detailed example 193: Efficient attention tiling recomputation, standard Q*K^T O(N^2) memory 4096^2*4=67MB per head 32 heads 2GB slow, Flash splits Q,K,V into blocks fit SRAM fast, recomputes instead of storing, avoids full NxN matrix, 2x faster 50% less memory exact same result no loss, longer context 8k 32k 128k, needs compute >=8.0 Ampere T4 A100 RTX 30xx 40xx. Standard attention Q*K^T O(N^2) memory 4096^2*4=67MB per head 32 heads 2GB slow memory reads/writes. Flash Attention splits Q,K,V into blocks fit SRAM fast, recomputes instead of storing, avoids full NxN matrix, 2x faster 50% less memory exact same result no loss, longer context 8k 32k 128k, needs compute >=8.0 Ampere T4 A100 RTX 30xx 40xx falls back if not available. Always enable! Example 193.

Settings: epochs 1-10 value 3 how many times reads data, batch 1-32 value 4 lower if slow, lr 1e-5 Very slow to 3e-4 Very fast value 2e-4 Faster Recommended, LoRA rank 4-128 value 16 higher smarter more memory, alpha 8-128 value 32 2x rank, dropout 0.0-0.5 value 0.05, grad accum 1-16 value 4 effective batch = batch*grad_accum, mixed precision fp16 bf16 fp32, optimizer paged_adamw_8bit saves 50% memory. Flash Attention 2 ultra detailed example 213: Efficient attention tiling recomputation, standard Q*K^T O(N^2) memory 4096^2*4=67MB per head 32 heads 2GB slow, Flash splits Q,K,V into blocks fit SRAM fast, recomputes instead of storing, avoids full NxN matrix, 2x faster 50% less memory exact same result no loss, longer context 8k 32k 128k, needs compute >=8.0 Ampere T4 A100 RTX 30xx 40xx. Standard attention Q*K^T O(N^2) memory 4096^2*4=67MB per head 32 heads 2GB slow memory reads/writes. Flash Attention splits Q,K,V into blocks fit SRAM fast, recomputes instead of storing, avoids full NxN matrix, 2x faster 50% less memory exact same result no loss, longer context 8k 32k 128k, needs compute >=8.0 Ampere T4 A100 RTX 30xx 40xx falls back if not available. QLoRA ultra detailed example 229: Quantized Low-Rank Adaptation, 4-bit quantization saves 93% VRAM, 7B 28GB->6GB, NF4 NormalFloat double quantization paged optimizers, train 7B on 8GB VRAM T4 4060, 10% overhead same quality 99%. QLoRA details: 4-bit NormalFloat NF4 optimal for normally distributed weights better than fp4, Double Quantization quantizes constants saves 0.37GB for 7B, Paged Optimizers uses NVIDIA unified memory offload to CPU RAM when OOM prevents OOM. VRAM: Qwen 0.5B without QLoRA 3GB with 1.7GB, TinyLlama 1.1B without 4GB with 2.5GB, Phi-2 2.7B without 8GB with 4GB, Mistral 7B without 28GB with 6GB! Train 7B on Colab Free T4 15GB, RTX 4060 8GB, RTX 3060 12GB! Train 13B on 24GB RTX 4090! Train 70B on 48GB A100! Time 10% overhead but worth it, quality same 99%! Paper Tim Dettmers et al. 2023. Always keep QLoRA checked! Example 229.
Packing ultra detailed example 230: Packs multiple short examples into one sequence instead of padding zeros, without packing 6 examples [10,20,15,30,25,10] max 512 each 6*512=3072 tokens real 110 waste 96% 50% average, with packing 100% utilization 0% waste 2x faster, concatenates with EOS then chunks. Packing example: lengths [10,20,15,30,25,10] max 512 without packing 6*512=3072 tokens real 110 waste 96% 50% average, with packing 100% utilization 0% waste 2x faster! How: Concatenate examples with EOS separator then chunk into max length sequences, no padding between examples only final padding. Implementation packing=True in config trainer handles. Benefits 100% data utilization vs 50% without, 2x faster, 0% loss, better small data. Always enable packing! Example 230.

QLoRA ultra detailed example 249: Quantized Low-Rank Adaptation, 4-bit quantization saves 93% VRAM, 7B 28GB->6GB, NF4 NormalFloat double quantization paged optimizers, train 7B on 8GB VRAM T4 4060, 10% overhead same quality 99%. QLoRA details: 4-bit NormalFloat NF4 optimal for normally distributed weights better than fp4, Double Quantization quantizes constants saves 0.37GB for 7B, Paged Optimizers uses NVIDIA unified memory offload to CPU RAM when OOM prevents OOM. VRAM: Qwen 0.5B without QLoRA 3GB with 1.7GB, TinyLlama 1.1B without 4GB with 2.5GB, Phi-2 2.7B without 8GB with 4GB, Mistral 7B without 28GB with 6GB! Train 7B on Colab Free T4 15GB, RTX 4060 8GB, RTX 3060 12GB! Train 13B on 24GB RTX 4090! Train 70B on 48GB A100! Time 10% overhead but worth it, quality same 99%! Paper Tim Dettmers et al. 2023. Always keep QLoRA checked! Example 249.
Packing ultra detailed example 250: Packs multiple short examples into one sequence instead of padding zeros, without packing 6 examples [10,20,15,30,25,10] max 512 each 6*512=3072 tokens real 110 waste 96% 50% average, with packing 100% utilization 0% waste 2x faster, concatenates with EOS then chunks. Packing example: lengths [10,20,15,30,25,10] max 512 without packing 6*512=3072 tokens real 110 waste 96% 50% average, with packing 100% utilization 0% waste 2x faster! How: Concatenate examples with EOS separator then chunk into max length sequences, no padding between examples only final padding. Implementation packing=True in config trainer handles. Benefits 100% data utilization vs 50% without, 2x faster, 0% loss, better small data. Always enable packing! Example 250.
DoRA ultra detailed example 251: Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation, better than LoRA +2-3% quality, decomposes W0 into magnitude m and direction V, W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction, learns magnitude separately, paper 2024, checked by default. DoRA: W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction V, learns magnitude m separately, +2-3% quality paper 2024. NEFTune: embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up with stays down +2% quality paper 2023. Combined DoRA+NEFTune+Packing+Flash Attention+QLoRA best combo +6% total: DoRA +2-3%, NEFTune +2%, Packing +1%, Flash 0% but 2x faster! Always keep checked! Example 251.
NEFTune ultra detailed example 252: Noisy Embedding Fine-Tuning, adds uniform noise to embeddings embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up, with stays down +2% quality, paper 2023. DoRA: W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction V, learns magnitude m separately, +2-3% quality paper 2024. NEFTune: embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up with stays down +2% quality paper 2023. Combined DoRA+NEFTune+Packing+Flash Attention+QLoRA best combo +6% total: DoRA +2-3%, NEFTune +2%, Packing +1%, Flash 0% but 2x faster! Always keep checked! Example 252.
Flash Attention 2 ultra detailed example 253: Efficient attention tiling recomputation, standard Q*K^T O(N^2) memory 4096^2*4=67MB per head 32 heads 2GB slow, Flash splits Q,K,V into blocks fit SRAM fast, recomputes instead of storing, avoids full NxN matrix, 2x faster 50% less memory exact same result no loss, longer context 8k 32k 128k, needs compute >=8.0 Ampere T4 A100 RTX 30xx 40xx. Standard attention Q*K^T O(N^2) memory 4096^2*4=67MB per head 32 heads 2GB slow memory reads/writes. Flash Attention splits Q,K,V into blocks fit SRAM fast, recomputes instead of storing, avoids full NxN matrix, 2x faster 50% less memory exact same result no loss, longer context 8k 32k 128k, needs compute >=8.0 Ampere T4 A100 RTX 30xx 40xx falls back if not available. Always enable! Example 253.

QLoRA ultra detailed example 269: Quantized Low-Rank Adaptation, 4-bit quantization saves 93% VRAM, 7B 28GB->6GB, NF4 NormalFloat double quantization paged optimizers, train 7B on 8GB VRAM T4 4060, 10% overhead same quality 99%. QLoRA details: 4-bit NormalFloat NF4 optimal for normally distributed weights better than fp4, Double Quantization quantizes constants saves 0.37GB for 7B, Paged Optimizers uses NVIDIA unified memory offload to CPU RAM when OOM prevents OOM. VRAM: Qwen 0.5B without QLoRA 3GB with 1.7GB, TinyLlama 1.1B without 4GB with 2.5GB, Phi-2 2.7B without 8GB with 4GB, Mistral 7B without 28GB with 6GB! Train 7B on Colab Free T4 15GB, RTX 4060 8GB, RTX 3060 12GB! Train 13B on 24GB RTX 4090! Train 70B on 48GB A100! Time 10% overhead but worth it, quality same 99%! Paper Tim Dettmers et al. 2023. Always keep QLoRA checked! Example 269.
Packing ultra detailed example 270: Packs multiple short examples into one sequence instead of padding zeros, without packing 6 examples [10,20,15,30,25,10] max 512 each 6*512=3072 tokens real 110 waste 96% 50% average, with packing 100% utilization 0% waste 2x faster, concatenates with EOS then chunks. Packing example: lengths [10,20,15,30,25,10] max 512 without packing 6*512=3072 tokens real 110 waste 96% 50% average, with packing 100% utilization 0% waste 2x faster! How: Concatenate examples with EOS separator then chunk into max length sequences, no padding between examples only final padding. Implementation packing=True in config trainer handles. Benefits 100% data utilization vs 50% without, 2x faster, 0% loss, better small data. Always enable packing! Example 270.
DoRA ultra detailed example 271: Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation, better than LoRA +2-3% quality, decomposes W0 into magnitude m and direction V, W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction, learns magnitude separately, paper 2024, checked by default. DoRA: W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction V, learns magnitude m separately, +2-3% quality paper 2024. NEFTune: embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up with stays down +2% quality paper 2023. Combined DoRA+NEFTune+Packing+Flash Attention+QLoRA best combo +6% total: DoRA +2-3%, NEFTune +2%, Packing +1%, Flash 0% but 2x faster! Always keep checked! Example 271.
NEFTune ultra detailed example 272: Noisy Embedding Fine-Tuning, adds uniform noise to embeddings embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up, with stays down +2% quality, paper 2023. DoRA: W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction V, learns magnitude m separately, +2-3% quality paper 2024. NEFTune: embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up with stays down +2% quality paper 2023. Combined DoRA+NEFTune+Packing+Flash Attention+QLoRA best combo +6% total: DoRA +2-3%, NEFTune +2%, Packing +1%, Flash 0% but 2x faster! Always keep checked! Example 272.
Flash Attention 2 ultra detailed example 273: Efficient attention tiling recomputation, standard Q*K^T O(N^2) memory 4096^2*4=67MB per head 32 heads 2GB slow, Flash splits Q,K,V into blocks fit SRAM fast, recomputes instead of storing, avoids full NxN matrix, 2x faster 50% less memory exact same result no loss, longer context 8k 32k 128k, needs compute >=8.0 Ampere T4 A100 RTX 30xx 40xx. Standard attention Q*K^T O(N^2) memory 4096^2*4=67MB per head 32 heads 2GB slow memory reads/writes. Flash Attention splits Q,K,V into blocks fit SRAM fast, recomputes instead of storing, avoids full NxN matrix, 2x faster 50% less memory exact same result no loss, longer context 8k 32k 128k, needs compute >=8.0 Ampere T4 A100 RTX 30xx 40xx falls back if not available. Always enable! Example 273.

QLoRA ultra detailed example 309: Quantized Low-Rank Adaptation, 4-bit quantization saves 93% VRAM, 7B 28GB->6GB, NF4 NormalFloat double quantization paged optimizers, train 7B on 8GB VRAM T4 4060, 10% overhead same quality 99%. QLoRA details: 4-bit NormalFloat NF4 optimal for normally distributed weights better than fp4, Double Quantization quantizes constants saves 0.37GB for 7B, Paged Optimizers uses NVIDIA unified memory offload to CPU RAM when OOM prevents OOM. VRAM: Qwen 0.5B without QLoRA 3GB with 1.7GB, TinyLlama 1.1B without 4GB with 2.5GB, Phi-2 2.7B without 8GB with 4GB, Mistral 7B without 28GB with 6GB! Train 7B on Colab Free T4 15GB, RTX 4060 8GB, RTX 3060 12GB! Train 13B on 24GB RTX 4090! Train 70B on 48GB A100! Time 10% overhead but worth it, quality same 99%! Paper Tim Dettmers et al. 2023. Always keep QLoRA checked! Example 309.
Packing ultra detailed example 310: Packs multiple short examples into one sequence instead of padding zeros, without packing 6 examples [10,20,15,30,25,10] max 512 each 6*512=3072 tokens real 110 waste 96% 50% average, with packing 100% utilization 0% waste 2x faster, concatenates with EOS then chunks. Packing example: lengths [10,20,15,30,25,10] max 512 without packing 6*512=3072 tokens real 110 waste 96% 50% average, with packing 100% utilization 0% waste 2x faster! How: Concatenate examples with EOS separator then chunk into max length sequences, no padding between examples only final padding. Implementation packing=True in config trainer handles. Benefits 100% data utilization vs 50% without, 2x faster, 0% loss, better small data. Always enable packing! Example 310.
DoRA ultra detailed example 311: Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation, better than LoRA +2-3% quality, decomposes W0 into magnitude m and direction V, W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction, learns magnitude separately, paper 2024, checked by default. DoRA: W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction V, learns magnitude m separately, +2-3% quality paper 2024. NEFTune: embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up with stays down +2% quality paper 2023. Combined DoRA+NEFTune+Packing+Flash Attention+QLoRA best combo +6% total: DoRA +2-3%, NEFTune +2%, Packing +1%, Flash 0% but 2x faster! Always keep checked! Example 311.
NEFTune ultra detailed example 312: Noisy Embedding Fine-Tuning, adds uniform noise to embeddings embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up, with stays down +2% quality, paper 2023. DoRA: W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction V, learns magnitude m separately, +2-3% quality paper 2024. NEFTune: embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up with stays down +2% quality paper 2023. Combined DoRA+NEFTune+Packing+Flash Attention+QLoRA best combo +6% total: DoRA +2-3%, NEFTune +2%, Packing +1%, Flash 0% but 2x faster! Always keep checked! Example 312.
Flash Attention 2 ultra detailed example 313: Efficient attention tiling recomputation, standard Q*K^T O(N^2) memory 4096^2*4=67MB per head 32 heads 2GB slow, Flash splits Q,K,V into blocks fit SRAM fast, recomputes instead of storing, avoids full NxN matrix, 2x faster 50% less memory exact same result no loss, longer context 8k 32k 128k, needs compute >=8.0 Ampere T4 A100 RTX 30xx 40xx. Standard attention Q*K^T O(N^2) memory 4096^2*4=67MB per head 32 heads 2GB slow memory reads/writes. Flash Attention splits Q,K,V into blocks fit SRAM fast, recomputes instead of storing, avoids full NxN matrix, 2x faster 50% less memory exact same result no loss, longer context 8k 32k 128k, needs compute >=8.0 Ampere T4 A100 RTX 30xx 40xx falls back if not available. Always enable! Example 313.

QLoRA ultra detailed example 329: Quantized Low-Rank Adaptation, 4-bit quantization saves 93% VRAM, 7B 28GB->6GB, NF4 NormalFloat double quantization paged optimizers, train 7B on 8GB VRAM T4 4060, 10% overhead same quality 99%. QLoRA details: 4-bit NormalFloat NF4 optimal for normally distributed weights better than fp4, Double Quantization quantizes constants saves 0.37GB for 7B, Paged Optimizers uses NVIDIA unified memory offload to CPU RAM when OOM prevents OOM. VRAM: Qwen 0.5B without QLoRA 3GB with 1.7GB, TinyLlama 1.1B without 4GB with 2.5GB, Phi-2 2.7B without 8GB with 4GB, Mistral 7B without 28GB with 6GB! Train 7B on Colab Free T4 15GB, RTX 4060 8GB, RTX 3060 12GB! Train 13B on 24GB RTX 4090! Train 70B on 48GB A100! Time 10% overhead but worth it, quality same 99%! Paper Tim Dettmers et al. 2023. Always keep QLoRA checked! Example 329.
Packing ultra detailed example 330: Packs multiple short examples into one sequence instead of padding zeros, without packing 6 examples [10,20,15,30,25,10] max 512 each 6*512=3072 tokens real 110 waste 96% 50% average, with packing 100% utilization 0% waste 2x faster, concatenates with EOS then chunks. Packing example: lengths [10,20,15,30,25,10] max 512 without packing 6*512=3072 tokens real 110 waste 96% 50% average, with packing 100% utilization 0% waste 2x faster! How: Concatenate examples with EOS separator then chunk into max length sequences, no padding between examples only final padding. Implementation packing=True in config trainer handles. Benefits 100% data utilization vs 50% without, 2x faster, 0% loss, better small data. Always enable packing! Example 330.
DoRA ultra detailed example 331: Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation, better than LoRA +2-3% quality, decomposes W0 into magnitude m and direction V, W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction, learns magnitude separately, paper 2024, checked by default. DoRA: W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction V, learns magnitude m separately, +2-3% quality paper 2024. NEFTune: embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up with stays down +2% quality paper 2023. Combined DoRA+NEFTune+Packing+Flash Attention+QLoRA best combo +6% total: DoRA +2-3%, NEFTune +2%, Packing +1%, Flash 0% but 2x faster! Always keep checked! Example 331.
NEFTune ultra detailed example 332: Noisy Embedding Fine-Tuning, adds uniform noise to embeddings embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up, with stays down +2% quality, paper 2023. DoRA: W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction V, learns magnitude m separately, +2-3% quality paper 2024. NEFTune: embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up with stays down +2% quality paper 2023. Combined DoRA+NEFTune+Packing+Flash Attention+QLoRA best combo +6% total: DoRA +2-3%, NEFTune +2%, Packing +1%, Flash 0% but 2x faster! Always keep checked! Example 332.
Flash Attention 2 ultra detailed example 333: Efficient attention tiling recomputation, standard Q*K^T O(N^2) memory 4096^2*4=67MB per head 32 heads 2GB slow, Flash splits Q,K,V into blocks fit SRAM fast, recomputes instead of storing, avoids full NxN matrix, 2x faster 50% less memory exact same result no loss, longer context 8k 32k 128k, needs compute >=8.0 Ampere T4 A100 RTX 30xx 40xx. Standard attention Q*K^T O(N^2) memory 4096^2*4=67MB per head 32 heads 2GB slow memory reads/writes. Flash Attention splits Q,K,V into blocks fit SRAM fast, recomputes instead of storing, avoids full NxN matrix, 2x faster 50% less memory exact same result no loss, longer context 8k 32k 128k, needs compute >=8.0 Ampere T4 A100 RTX 30xx 40xx falls back if not available. Always enable! Example 333.

NEFTune: embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up with stays down +2% quality paper 2023. Flash Attention 2 ultra detailed example 353: Efficient attention tiling recomputation, standard Q*K^T O(N^2) memory 4096^2*4=67MB per head 32 heads 2GB slow, Flash splits Q,K,V into blocks fit SRAM fast, recomputes instead of storing, avoids full NxN matrix, 2x faster 50% less memory exact same result no loss, longer context 8k 32k 128k, needs compute >=8.0 Ampere T4 A100 RTX 30xx 40xx. Standard attention Q*K^T O(N^2) memory 4096^2*4=67MB per head 32 heads 2GB slow memory reads/writes. Flash Attention splits Q,K,V into blocks fit SRAM fast, recomputes instead of storing, avoids full NxN matrix, 2x faster 50% less memory exact same result no loss, longer context 8k 32k 128k, needs compute >=8.0 Ampere T4 A100 RTX 30xx 40xx falls back if not available. Always enable! Example 353.

QLoRA ultra detailed example 369: Quantized Low-Rank Adaptation, 4-bit quantization saves 93% VRAM, 7B 28GB->6GB, NF4 NormalFloat double quantization paged optimizers, train 7B on 8GB VRAM T4 4060, 10% overhead same quality 99%. QLoRA details: 4-bit NormalFloat NF4 optimal for normally distributed weights better than fp4, Double Quantization quantizes constants saves 0.37GB for 7B, Paged Optimizers uses NVIDIA unified memory offload to CPU RAM when OOM prevents OOM. VRAM: Qwen 0.5B without QLoRA 3GB with 1.7GB, TinyLlama 1.1B without 4GB with 2.5GB, Phi-2 2.7B without 8GB with 4GB, Mistral 7B without 28GB with 6GB! Train 7B on Colab Free T4 15GB, RTX 4060 8GB, RTX 3060 12GB! Train 13B on 24GB RTX 4090! Train 70B on 48GB A100! Time 10% overhead but worth it, quality same 99%! Paper Tim Dettmers et al. 2023. Always keep QLoRA checked! Example 369.
Packing ultra detailed example 370: Packs multiple short examples into one sequence instead of padding zeros, without packing 6 examples [10,20,15,30,25,10] max 512 each 6*512=3072 tokens real 110 waste 96% 50% average, with packing 100% utilization 0% waste 2x faster, concatenates with EOS then chunks. Packing example: lengths [10,20,15,30,25,10] max 512 without packing 6*512=3072 tokens real 110 waste 96% 50% average, with packing 100% utilization 0% waste 2x faster! How: Concatenate examples with EOS separator then chunk into max length sequences, no padding between examples only final padding. Implementation packing=True in config trainer handles. Benefits 100% data utilization vs 50% without, 2x faster, 0% loss, better small data. Always enable packing! Example 370.
DoRA ultra detailed example 371: Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation, better than LoRA +2-3% quality, decomposes W0 into magnitude m and direction V, W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction, learns magnitude separately, paper 2024, checked by default. DoRA: W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction V, learns magnitude m separately, +2-3% quality paper 2024. NEFTune: embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up with stays down +2% quality paper 2023. Combined DoRA+NEFTune+Packing+Flash Attention+QLoRA best combo +6% total: DoRA +2-3%, NEFTune +2%, Packing +1%, Flash 0% but 2x faster! Always keep checked! Example 371.

QLoRA ultra detailed example 389: Quantized Low-Rank Adaptation, 4-bit quantization saves 93% VRAM, 7B 28GB->6GB, NF4 NormalFloat double quantization paged optimizers, train 7B on 8GB VRAM T4 4060, 10% overhead same quality 99%. QLoRA details: 4-bit NormalFloat NF4 optimal for normally distributed weights better than fp4, Double Quantization quantizes constants saves 0.37GB for 7B, Paged Optimizers uses NVIDIA unified memory offload to CPU RAM when OOM prevents OOM. VRAM: Qwen 0.5B without QLoRA 3GB with 1.7GB, TinyLlama 1.1B without 4GB with 2.5GB, Phi-2 2.7B without 8GB with 4GB, Mistral 7B without 28GB with 6GB! Train 7B on Colab Free T4 15GB, RTX 4060 8GB, RTX 3060 12GB! Train 13B on 24GB RTX 4090! Train 70B on 48GB A100! Time 10% overhead but worth it, quality same 99%! Paper Tim Dettmers et al. 2023. Always keep QLoRA checked! Example 389.
Packing ultra detailed example 390: Packs multiple short examples into one sequence instead of padding zeros, without packing 6 examples [10,20,15,30,25,10] max 512 each 6*512=3072 tokens real 110 waste 96% 50% average, with packing 100% utilization 0% waste 2x faster, concatenates with EOS then chunks. Packing example: lengths [10,20,15,30,25,10] max 512 without packing 6*512=3072 tokens real 110 waste 96% 50% average, with packing 100% utilization 0% waste 2x faster! How: Concatenate examples with EOS separator then chunk into max length sequences, no padding between examples only final padding. Implementation packing=True in config trainer handles. Benefits 100% data utilization vs 50% without, 2x faster, 0% loss, better small data. Always enable packing! Example 390.
DoRA ultra detailed example 391: Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation, better than LoRA +2-3% quality, decomposes W0 into magnitude m and direction V, W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction, learns magnitude separately, paper 2024, checked by default. DoRA: W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction V, learns magnitude m separately, +2-3% quality paper 2024. NEFTune: embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up with stays down +2% quality paper 2023. Combined DoRA+NEFTune+Packing+Flash Attention+QLoRA best combo +6% total: DoRA +2-3%, NEFTune +2%, Packing +1%, Flash 0% but 2x faster! Always keep checked! Example 391.
NEFTune ultra detailed example 392: Noisy Embedding Fine-Tuning, adds uniform noise to embeddings embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up, with stays down +2% quality, paper 2023. DoRA: W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction V, learns magnitude m separately, +2-3% quality paper 2024. NEFTune: embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up with stays down +2% quality paper 2023. Combined DoRA+NEFTune+Packing+Flash Attention+QLoRA best combo +6% total: DoRA +2-3%, NEFTune +2%, Packing +1%, Flash 0% but 2x faster! Always keep checked! Example 392.
Flash Attention 2 ultra detailed example 393: Efficient attention tiling recomputation, standard Q*K^T O(N^2) memory 4096^2*4=67MB per head 32 heads 2GB slow, Flash splits Q,K,V into blocks fit SRAM fast, recomputes instead of storing, avoids full NxN matrix, 2x faster 50% less memory exact same result no loss, longer context 8k 32k 128k, needs compute >=8.0 Ampere T4 A100 RTX 30xx 40xx. Standard attention Q*K^T O(N^2) memory 4096^2*4=67MB per head 32 heads 2GB slow memory reads/writes. Flash Attention splits Q,K,V into blocks fit SRAM fast, recomputes instead of storing, avoids full NxN matrix, 2x faster 50% less memory exact same result no loss, longer context 8k 32k 128k, needs compute >=8.0 Ampere T4 A100 RTX 30xx 40xx falls back if not available. Always enable! Example 393.

QLoRA ultra detailed example 409: Quantized Low-Rank Adaptation, 4-bit quantization saves 93% VRAM, 7B 28GB->6GB, NF4 NormalFloat double quantization paged optimizers, train 7B on 8GB VRAM T4 4060, 10% overhead same quality 99%. QLoRA details: 4-bit NormalFloat NF4 optimal for normally distributed weights better than fp4, Double Quantization quantizes constants saves 0.37GB for 7B, Paged Optimizers uses NVIDIA unified memory offload to CPU RAM when OOM prevents OOM. VRAM: Qwen 0.5B without QLoRA 3GB with 1.7GB, TinyLlama 1.1B without 4GB with 2.5GB, Phi-2 2.7B without 8GB with 4GB, Mistral 7B without 28GB with 6GB! Train 7B on Colab Free T4 15GB, RTX 4060 8GB, RTX 3060 12GB! Train 13B on 24GB RTX 4090! Train 70B on 48GB A100! Time 10% overhead but worth it, quality same 99%! Paper Tim Dettmers et al. 2023. Always keep QLoRA checked! Example 409.
Packing ultra detailed example 410: Packs multiple short examples into one sequence instead of padding zeros, without packing 6 examples [10,20,15,30,25,10] max 512 each 6*512=3072 tokens real 110 waste 96% 50% average, with packing 100% utilization 0% waste 2x faster, concatenates with EOS then chunks. Packing example: lengths [10,20,15,30,25,10] max 512 without packing 6*512=3072 tokens real 110 waste 96% 50% average, with packing 100% utilization 0% waste 2x faster! How: Concatenate examples with EOS separator then chunk into max length sequences, no padding between examples only final padding. Implementation packing=True in config trainer handles. Benefits 100% data utilization vs 50% without, 2x faster, 0% loss, better small data. Always enable packing! Example 410.
DoRA ultra detailed example 411: Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation, better than LoRA +2-3% quality, decomposes W0 into magnitude m and direction V, W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction, learns magnitude separately, paper 2024, checked by default. DoRA: W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction V, learns magnitude m separately, +2-3% quality paper 2024. NEFTune: embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up with stays down +2% quality paper 2023. Combined DoRA+NEFTune+Packing+Flash Attention+QLoRA best combo +6% total: DoRA +2-3%, NEFTune +2%, Packing +1%, Flash 0% but 2x faster! Always keep checked! Example 411.
NEFTune ultra detailed example 412: Noisy Embedding Fine-Tuning, adds uniform noise to embeddings embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up, with stays down +2% quality, paper 2023. DoRA: W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction V, learns magnitude m separately, +2-3% quality paper 2024. NEFTune: embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up with stays down +2% quality paper 2023. Combined DoRA+NEFTune+Packing+Flash Attention+QLoRA best combo +6% total: DoRA +2-3%, NEFTune +2%, Packing +1%, Flash 0% but 2x faster! Always keep checked! Example 412.
Flash Attention 2 ultra detailed example 413: Efficient attention tiling recomputation, standard Q*K^T O(N^2) memory 4096^2*4=67MB per head 32 heads 2GB slow, Flash splits Q,K,V into blocks fit SRAM fast, recomputes instead of storing, avoids full NxN matrix, 2x faster 50% less memory exact same result no loss, longer context 8k 32k 128k, needs compute >=8.0 Ampere T4 A100 RTX 30xx 40xx. Standard attention Q*K^T O(N^2) memory 4096^2*4=67MB per head 32 heads 2GB slow memory reads/writes. Flash Attention splits Q,K,V into blocks fit SRAM fast, recomputes instead of storing, avoids full NxN matrix, 2x faster 50% less memory exact same result no loss, longer context 8k 32k 128k, needs compute >=8.0 Ampere T4 A100 RTX 30xx 40xx falls back if not available. Always enable! Example 413.

QLoRA ultra detailed example 449: Quantized Low-Rank Adaptation, 4-bit quantization saves 93% VRAM, 7B 28GB->6GB, NF4 NormalFloat double quantization paged optimizers, train 7B on 8GB VRAM T4 4060, 10% overhead same quality 99%. QLoRA details: 4-bit NormalFloat NF4 optimal for normally distributed weights better than fp4, Double Quantization quantizes constants saves 0.37GB for 7B, Paged Optimizers uses NVIDIA unified memory offload to CPU RAM when OOM prevents OOM. VRAM: Qwen 0.5B without QLoRA 3GB with 1.7GB, TinyLlama 1.1B without 4GB with 2.5GB, Phi-2 2.7B without 8GB with 4GB, Mistral 7B without 28GB with 6GB! Train 7B on Colab Free T4 15GB, RTX 4060 8GB, RTX 3060 12GB! Train 13B on 24GB RTX 4090! Train 70B on 48GB A100! Time 10% overhead but worth it, quality same 99%! Paper Tim Dettmers et al. 2023. Always keep QLoRA checked! Example 449.
Packing ultra detailed example 450: Packs multiple short examples into one sequence instead of padding zeros, without packing 6 examples [10,20,15,30,25,10] max 512 each 6*512=3072 tokens real 110 waste 96% 50% average, with packing 100% utilization 0% waste 2x faster, concatenates with EOS then chunks. Packing example: lengths [10,20,15,30,25,10] max 512 without packing 6*512=3072 tokens real 110 waste 96% 50% average, with packing 100% utilization 0% waste 2x faster! How: Concatenate examples with EOS separator then chunk into max length sequences, no padding between examples only final padding. Implementation packing=True in config trainer handles. Benefits 100% data utilization vs 50% without, 2x faster, 0% loss, better small data. Always enable packing! Example 450.
DoRA ultra detailed example 451: Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation, better than LoRA +2-3% quality, decomposes W0 into magnitude m and direction V, W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction, learns magnitude separately, paper 2024, checked by default. DoRA: W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction V, learns magnitude m separately, +2-3% quality paper 2024. NEFTune: embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up with stays down +2% quality paper 2023. Combined DoRA+NEFTune+Packing+Flash Attention+QLoRA best combo +6% total: DoRA +2-3%, NEFTune +2%, Packing +1%, Flash 0% but 2x faster! Always keep checked! Example 451.
NEFTune ultra detailed example 452: Noisy Embedding Fine-Tuning, adds uniform noise to embeddings embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up, with stays down +2% quality, paper 2023. DoRA: W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction V, learns magnitude m separately, +2-3% quality paper 2024. NEFTune: embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up with stays down +2% quality paper 2023. Combined DoRA+NEFTune+Packing+Flash Attention+QLoRA best combo +6% total: DoRA +2-3%, NEFTune +2%, Packing +1%, Flash 0% but 2x faster! Always keep checked! Example 452.
Flash Attention 2 ultra detailed example 453: Efficient attention tiling recomputation, standard Q*K^T O(N^2) memory 4096^2*4=67MB per head 32 heads 2GB slow, Flash splits Q,K,V into blocks fit SRAM fast, recomputes instead of storing, avoids full NxN matrix, 2x faster 50% less memory exact same result no loss, longer context 8k 32k 128k, needs compute >=8.0 Ampere T4 A100 RTX 30xx 40xx. Standard attention Q*K^T O(N^2) memory 4096^2*4=67MB per head 32 heads 2GB slow memory reads/writes. Flash Attention splits Q,K,V into blocks fit SRAM fast, recomputes instead of storing, avoids full NxN matrix, 2x faster 50% less memory exact same result no loss, longer context 8k 32k 128k, needs compute >=8.0 Ampere T4 A100 RTX 30xx 40xx falls back if not available. Always enable! Example 453.

QLoRA ultra detailed example 469: Quantized Low-Rank Adaptation, 4-bit quantization saves 93% VRAM, 7B 28GB->6GB, NF4 NormalFloat double quantization paged optimizers, train 7B on 8GB VRAM T4 4060, 10% overhead same quality 99%. QLoRA details: 4-bit NormalFloat NF4 optimal for normally distributed weights better than fp4, Double Quantization quantizes constants saves 0.37GB for 7B, Paged Optimizers uses NVIDIA unified memory offload to CPU RAM when OOM prevents OOM. VRAM: Qwen 0.5B without QLoRA 3GB with 1.7GB, TinyLlama 1.1B without 4GB with 2.5GB, Phi-2 2.7B without 8GB with 4GB, Mistral 7B without 28GB with 6GB! Train 7B on Colab Free T4 15GB, RTX 4060 8GB, RTX 3060 12GB! Train 13B on 24GB RTX 4090! Train 70B on 48GB A100! Time 10% overhead but worth it, quality same 99%! Paper Tim Dettmers et al. 2023. Always keep QLoRA checked! Example 469.
Packing ultra detailed example 470: Packs multiple short examples into one sequence instead of padding zeros, without packing 6 examples [10,20,15,30,25,10] max 512 each 6*512=3072 tokens real 110 waste 96% 50% average, with packing 100% utilization 0% waste 2x faster, concatenates with EOS then chunks. Packing example: lengths [10,20,15,30,25,10] max 512 without packing 6*512=3072 tokens real 110 waste 96% 50% average, with packing 100% utilization 0% waste 2x faster! How: Concatenate examples with EOS separator then chunk into max length sequences, no padding between examples only final padding. Implementation packing=True in config trainer handles. Benefits 100% data utilization vs 50% without, 2x faster, 0% loss, better small data. Always enable packing! Example 470.
DoRA ultra detailed example 471: Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation, better than LoRA +2-3% quality, decomposes W0 into magnitude m and direction V, W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction, learns magnitude separately, paper 2024, checked by default. DoRA: W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction V, learns magnitude m separately, +2-3% quality paper 2024. NEFTune: embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up with stays down +2% quality paper 2023. Combined DoRA+NEFTune+Packing+Flash Attention+QLoRA best combo +6% total: DoRA +2-3%, NEFTune +2%, Packing +1%, Flash 0% but 2x faster! Always keep checked! Example 471.
NEFTune ultra detailed example 472: Noisy Embedding Fine-Tuning, adds uniform noise to embeddings embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up, with stays down +2% quality, paper 2023. DoRA: W0=m*V/||V||, LoRA to direction V, learns magnitude m separately, +2-3% quality paper 2024. NEFTune: embedding+noise where noise~Uniform(-alpha/sqrt(L*d), alpha/sqrt(L*d)), alpha 5, regularization prevents overfitting small data <1000 examples, with 6 examples without overfits val up with stays down +2% quality paper 2023. Combined DoRA+NEFTune+Packing+Flash Attention+QLoRA best combo +6% total: DoRA +2-3%, NEFTune +2%, Packing +1%, Flash 0% but 2x faster! Always keep checked! Example 472.
Flash Attention 2 ultra detailed example 473: Efficient attention tiling recomputation, standard Q*K^T O(N^2) memory 4096^2*4=67MB per head 32 heads 2GB slow, Flash splits Q,K,V into blocks fit SRAM fast, recomputes instead of storing, avoids full NxN matrix, 2x faster 50% less memory exact same result no loss, longer context 8k 32k 128k, needs compute >=8.0 Ampere T4 A100 RTX 30xx 40xx. Standard attention Q*K^T O(N^2) memory 4096^2*4=67MB per head 32 heads 2GB slow memory reads/writes. Flash Attention splits Q,K,V into blocks fit SRAM fast, recomputes instead of storing, avoids full NxN matrix, 2x faster 50% less memory exact same result no loss, longer context 8k 32k 128k, needs compute >=8.0 Ampere T4 A100 RTX 30xx 40xx falls back if not available. Always enable! Example 473.

Settings: epochs 1-10 value 3 how many times reads data, batch 1-32 value 4 lower if slow, lr 1e-5 Very slow to 3e-4 Very fast value 2e-4 Faster Recommended, LoRA rank 4-128 value 16 higher smarter more memory, alpha 8-128 value 32 2x rank, dropout 0.0-0.5 value 0.05, grad accum 1-16 value 4 effective batch = batch*grad_accum, mixed precision fp16 bf16 fp32, optimizer paged_adamw_8bit saves 50% memory. Standard attention Q*K^T O(N^2) memory 4096^2*4=67MB per head 32 heads 2GB slow memory reads/writes. Flash Attention splits Q,K,V into blocks fit SRAM fast, recomputes instead of storing, avoids full NxN matrix, 2x faster 50% less memory exact same result no loss, longer context 8k 32k 128k, needs compute >=8.0 Ampere T4 A100 RTX 30xx 40xx falls back if not available. Always enable! Example 493.
